	public float GetKnockbackMultiplier_ScaledByDifficulty(float normalKnockbackMult)
	{
		return normalKnockbackMult * GameDifficultyData.KnockbackToEnemiesMultiplier.Sample(difficulty);
	}

	public int GetAttackDamage_ScaledByDifficulty(float normalDamage)
	{
		return (int)(normalDamage * GameDifficultyData.EnemyDamageMultiplier.Sample(difficulty));
	}

	public int GetAttackDamage_CappedAtMaster(float normalDamage)
	{
		float num = Math.Min(difficulty, GameDifficultyLevel.Master);
		return (int)(normalDamage * GameDifficultyData.EnemyDamageMultiplier.Sample(num));
	}

	public int GetAttackDamage_LerpBetweenFinalValues(float normalDamage, float expertDamage)
	{
		return (int)Utils.Remap(difficulty, GameDifficultyLevel.Classic, GameDifficultyLevel.Expert, normalDamage, expertDamage);
	}

	public int GetAttackDamage_ForProjectiles(float normalDamage, float expertDamage)
	{
		return (int)Utils.Remap(difficulty, GameDifficultyLevel.Classic, GameDifficultyLevel.Expert, normalDamage, expertDamage);
	}

	public int GetAttackDamage_ForProjectiles_MultiLerp(float normalDamage, float expertDamage, float masterDamage)
	{
		return (int)Utils.MultiLerp(Utils.GetLerpValue(GameDifficultyLevel.Classic, GameDifficultyLevel.Master, difficulty, clamped: true), normalDamage, expertDamage, masterDamage);
	}

179	/** GetKnockbackMultiplier_ScaledByDifficulty（:7010） */
180	export function getKnockbackMultiplierScaledByDifficulty(normalKnockbackMult: number, difficulty: number): number {

184	/** GetAttackDamage_ScaledByDifficulty（:7015）：(int)(normal * EnemyDamageMultiplier.Sample(difficulty)) */
185	export function getAttackDamageScaledByDifficulty(normalDamage: number, difficulty: number): number {
186	  return Math.trunc(f32(normalDamage * sampleCurve(ENEMY_DAMAGE_MULTIPLIER, difficulty)));
187	}
188	
189	/** GetAttackDamage_CappedAtMaster（:7020）：大师以上钳在 Master 档 */
190	export function getAttackDamageCappedAtMaster(normalDamage: number, difficulty: number): number {
191	  const num = Math.min(difficulty, GDL.MASTER);
192	  return Math.trunc(f32(normalDamage * sampleCurve(ENEMY_DAMAGE_MULTIPLIER, num)));
193	}

195	/** GetAttackDamage_ForProjectiles（:7031）：Remap(difficulty, Classic, Expert, normal, expert) */
196	export function getAttackDamageForProjectiles(normalDamage: number, expertDamage: number, difficulty: number): number {
197	  return Math.trunc(remap(difficulty, GDL.CLASSIC, GDL.EXPERT, normalDamage, expertDamage));
198	}

203	export function hostileProjectileDamage(damage: number, difficulty: number): number {

18	// 仅 ScaleStats_ForExpertHardmode 对 boss 提前 return，:18471-18474）。
19	// 本模块纯函数化（状态全走入参/出参 bundle），世界上下文经 bindScaleStatsWorld
20	// 注入（Game.afterWorldLoad；对应原版 Main 静态单例）。
21	// ============================================================================
22	import type { World } from '../world/World';
23	
24	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
25	// GameDifficultyLevel（GameDifficultyLevel.cs:8-16）：档位是【浮点值】不是枚举序号
26	//   Journey 0.5 / Classic 1 / Expert 2 / Master 3 / Legendary 4
27	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
28	export const GDL = {
29	  JOURNEY: 0.5,
30	  CLASSIC: 1,
31	  EXPERT: 2,
32	  MASTER: 3,
33	  LEGENDARY: 4,
34	} as const;
35	
36	/** float32（C# float 字面量/强转位）。JS number 是 float64，在原版显式 (float) 处对齐 */
37	const f32 = (v: number): number => Math.fround(v);
38	
39	/** C# Math.Round(double)（默认 MidpointRounding.ToEven 银行家舍入：.5 → 就近偶数；
40	 *  JS Math.round 是 .5 远离零舍入，逐半值处会差 1） */
41	export const roundCS = (v: number): number => {
42	  const f = Math.floor(v);
43	  const d = v - f;
44	  if (d < 0.5) return f;
45	  if (d > 0.5) return f + 1;
46	  return f % 2 === 0 ? f : f + 1;
47	};
48	
49	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
50	// Utils.GetLerpValue / Remap（Utils.cs:283-320，Remap 重载默认 clamped=true）
51	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
52	export function getLerpValue(from: number, to: number, t: number, clamped = false): number {
53	  if (clamped) {
54	    if (from < to) {
55	      if (t < from) return 0;
56	      if (t > to) return 1;
57	    } else {
58	      if (t < to) return 1;
59	      if (t > from) return 0;
60	    }
61	  }
62	  return (t - from) / (to - from);
63	}
64	
65	/** MathHelper.Lerp（XNA：a + (b-a)*t） */
66	export const lerpF = (a: number, b: number, t: number): number => f32(a + f32(b - a) * t);
67	
68	/** Utils.Lerp(double)（:246：a + (b-a)*t，双精度）——ByPlayerCount 的 balance 混合用 */
69	export const lerp = (a: number, b: number, t: number): number => a + (b - a) * t;
70	
71	/** Utils.Remap（:313，默认 clamped=true）——float 重载入参先 (float) 对齐 */
72	export function remap(fromValue: number, fromMin: number, fromMax: number, toMin: number, toMax: number): number {
73	  return lerpF(f32(toMin), f32(toMax), getLerpValue(f32(fromMin), f32(fromMax), f32(fromValue), true));
74	}
75	
76	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
77	// GameDifficultyData.LinearCurve（GameDifficultyData.cs:16-60）：
78	//   keys 升序；Sample 找到 value 落入的相邻键区间做线性插值，
79	//   ≤首键输入取首键输出、≥尾键输入取尾键输出
80	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
81	export interface CurveKey { input: number; output: number }
82	
83	export function sampleCurve(keys: readonly CurveKey[], value: number): number {
84	  let key = keys[0];
85	  let key2 = key;
86	  for (let i = 0; i < keys.length; i++) {
87	    key2 = keys[i];
88	    if (value <= key2.input) break;
89	    key = key2;
90	  }
91	  const num = f32(key2.input - key.input);
92	  const num2 = f32(key2.output - key.output);
93	  if (num === 0) return key.output;
94	  return f32(f32(f32(value - key.input) * num2) / num + key.output);
95	}

103	];

106	];

109	];

112	  { input: GDL.MASTER, output: 2.5 }, { input: GDL.LEGENDARY, output: 3.5 },

122	//   我方 world.difficulty：0 经典 / 1 专家 / 2 大师 / 3 旅程（World.ts:73）。
123	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
124	export function mainDifficulty(world: Pick<World, 'difficulty' | 'seedFlags'> | null | undefined): number {
125	  let num: number = GDL.CLASSIC;
126	  if (world) {
127	    if (world.difficulty === 1) num = GDL.EXPERT;
128	    else if (world.difficulty === 2) num = GDL.MASTER;
129	    if (world.seedFlags?.getGoodWorld) num += 1;
130	  }

Now I understand the current helpers. Key observations for item 8 (rotation fix): `applyEocRotation` uses `diff * Math.min(1, rate * 10)` — an exponential lerp, not vanilla's linear step. So fix: linear step ±rate with snap band. Also remove the wrong "case 0 0.05 档"? Wait, the task says "删 case 0 的错配 0.05 档(NPC.cs:19975-20012)". Hmm — current code: `let rotRate = 0.02;` and in case 0 `if (this.aiT > 40) rotRate = 0.05;`. Vanilla rotRate table:
- ai[0]==0 && ai[1]==0 (P1 hover): 0.02 — no time condition
- ai[0]==0 && ai[1]==2 && ai[2]>40 (P1 dash after 40t): 0.05
- ai[0]==3 && ai[1]==0 (P2 hover): 0.05
- ai[0]==3 && ai[1]==2 && ai[2]>40 (P2 dash): 0.08
- ai[0]==3 && ai[1]==4 && ai[2]>num4 (chain dash): 0.15
- ai[0]==3 && ai[1]==5 (enrage hover): 0.05
- expert: ×1.5; flag3&&expert: 0

So in case 0 (hover), the `aiT > 40 → 0.05` is wrong: P1 hover should stay 0.02; P2 hover is 0.05 regardless. And in case 2 (dash), rate should be 0 (vanilla sets rotation directly to atan2(v)-1.57 while ai[2]<40, no lerp). Actually vanilla: during dash (ai[1]==2), if ai[2] >= 40 → friction (rotation unchanged), else rotation = atan2(vel) − 1.57 (direct set). So case 2 shouldn't lerp at all. Current code does direct set in case 2 (`this.visAngle = atan2 - π/2`) — good, and excludes state 2 from the trailing applyEocRotation. OK.

So rotation fix:
- rotRate per state table with expert ×1.5 and flag3 → 0.
- Linear step with snap band in applyEocRotation.
- Remove case 0's `if (this.aiT > 40) rotRate = 0.05;` (that condition belongs to dash state).

Also note: vanilla hover target num7 = atan2(dyFromEoCToPlayer... wait num7 is computed from num5 = EoC.x - player.x (vector from player to EoC), num6 = EoC.bottomY - 59 - player.centerY. So num7 = atan2(EoC→player offset reversed) + 1.57 = angle from player to EoC + π/2. Current code: `Math.atan2(dy, dx) - Math.PI * 0.5` where dx,dy = player - EoC. atan2(player-EoC) = atan2(-(EoC-player)) = atan2(EoC→player vector) + π (mod 2π). So atan2(dy,dx) − π/2 ≡ atan2(EoC−player) + π − π/2 = atan2(EoC−player) + π/2. Equivalent to num7. Good — current formula matches vanilla num7.

Also note the render: does the repo draw rotation as `visAngle` directly? For the snap band I need to replicate vanilla's exact behavior:
```
if (rotation < num7) { if (num7 - rotation > 3.1415) rotation -= num8; else rotation += num8; }
else if (rotation > num7) { if (rotation - num7 > 3.1415) rotation += num8; else rotation -= num8; }
// wrap to [0, 6.283)
if (rotation > num7 - num8 && rotation < num7 + num8) rotation = num7;
```

## 🤖 Assistant · 2026-08-13T00:47:36.068Z · glm-x-preview-260804

<details open>
<summary>💭 thinking</summary>

```
Found it: line 2305 `this.visAngle = Math.atan2(fay, fax) + Math.PI * 0.5;` — with comment at 2301: "+π/2 为该族贴图正面朝上的实测符号（原版 −π/2 叠默认镜像等价）".

Item 5: "仆从朝向 180° 反(NPC.cs:51043-51046):AI_005 转头族(Enemy.ts:2300 附近)visAngle 改 atan2−π/2(球体朝前);Renderer rotationDriven 判定核对。"

Vanilla: `rotation = atan2(velocity.Y, velocity.X) - 1.57f` for the else-family (including servant 5). And for face-target family (6/94/173/619): `rotation = atan2(num5, num4) - 1.57f` where num4/num5 = direction to target (dx/dy quantized). Vanilla is −1.57 for both. The repo uses +π/2 with a claim that the texture's front faces up.

The claim "+π/2 为该族贴图正面朝上的实测符号(原版 −π/2 叠默认镜像等价)" — for canvas rendering: `ctx.rotate(angle)` with positive = clockwise (Y down). XNA SpriteBatch rotation positive is also clockwise. And the default spriteDirection = -1 means the sprite is drawn flipped horizontally by default in vanilla (DrawNPC applies SpriteEffects.FacedHorizontally when spriteDirection==-1). 86	export class Sfx {
87	  private ctx: AudioContext | null = null;
88	  private buffers = new Map<string, AudioBuffer>();
89	  /** 在途解码中的文件（防重复 fetch） */
90	  private pending = new Set<string>();
91	  /** 取回失败/不存在的文件（负缓存，防 404 每次重试） */
92	  private failed = new Set<string>();
93	  muted = false;
94	  /** 主音量 0..1（M6 设置页） */
95	  master = 1;
96	
97	  /** 按需取缓冲：未命中时后台 fetch+decode，同步返回已就位缓冲或 null */
98	  private ensureBuffer(file: string): AudioBuffer | null {
99	    const buf = this.buffers.get(file);
100	    if (buf) return buf;
101	    if (this.pending.has(file) || this.failed.has(file)) return null;
102	    const ctx = this.ensure(true);
103	    if (!ctx) return null;
104	    this.pending.add(file);
105	    fetch(`sounds/${file}.wav`)
106	      .then((r) => (r.ok ? r.arrayBuffer() : null))
107	      .then((ab) => (ab ? ctx.decodeAudioData(ab) : null))
108	      .then((decoded) => {
109	        if (decoded) this.buffers.set(file, decoded);
110	        else this.failed.add(file);
111	      })
112	      .catch(() => { this.failed.add(file); })
113	      .finally(() => { this.pending.delete(file); });
114	    return null;
115	  }
116	
117	  /** 定向预热（不阻塞）：把指定 wav 后台取齐，播放时即命中 */
118	  preloadFiles(files: Iterable<string>) {
119	    for (const f of files) this.ensureBuffer(f);
120	  }
121	
122	  /** 按逻辑名预热（WAV_MAP 展开到具体文件） */
123	  preloadNames(names: SfxName[]) {
124	    for (const n of names) for (const f of WAV_MAP[n] ?? []) this.ensureBuffer(f);
125	  }
126	
127	  // ---- 雨声层：原版 RainAmbience（Music_28.mp3，XACT Wave Bank 同源提取）循环 ----
128	  private rainGain: GainNode | null = null;
129	  private rainSrc: AudioBufferSourceNode | null = null;
130	  private rainLoaded = false;
131	  /** 设置雨声强度 0-1（每帧调用；0 时静音不拆节点——重启有冷启动开销） */
132	  setRain(vol: number) {
133	    const ctx = this.ensure(true);
134	    if (!ctx) return;
135	    if (!this.rainGain) {
136	      const g = ctx.createGain();
137	      g.gain.value = 0;
138	      g.connect(ctx.destination);
139	      this.rainGain = g;
140	      // 原版雨环境音轨（RainAmbience）懒加载循环；加载失败静默（雨声可选）
141	      fetch('audios/music/Music_28.mp3')
142	        .then((r) => (r.ok ? r.arrayBuffer() : null))
143	        .then((ab) => (ab ? ctx.decodeAudioData(ab) : null))
144	        .then((buf) => {
145	          if (!buf) return;
146	          const src = ctx.createBufferSource();
147	          src.buffer = buf;
148	          src.loop = true;
149	          src.connect(this.rainGain!);
150	          src.start();
151	          this.rainSrc = src;
152	          this.rainLoaded = true;
153	        })
154	        .catch(() => {});
155	    }
156	    const v = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, vol)) * this.master * 0.6;
157	    this.rainGain.gain.setTargetAtTime(v, ctx.currentTime, 0.4);
158	  }
159	
160	  /** 世界声距离衰减（LegacySoundPlayer.cs:160 SoundAttenuationDistance = 2500px） */
161	  static readonly ATTEN = 2500;
162	  /** 监听器（原版 Main.Camera.Center，相机中心非玩家）；Game 每帧更新。未进世界=(0,0) */
163	  private lx = 0; private ly = 0;
164	  setListener(x: number, y: number) { this.lx = x; this.ly = y; }
165	  /** 原版距离衰减（LegacySoundPlayer.cs:381-401）：dist ≥2500 → 不播；
166	   *  vol × (1 - dist/2500)。不传坐标（x=-1 语义）→ 全量（UI/玩家自身动作声） */
167	  private atten(vol: number, x?: number, y?: number): number {
168	    if (x == null || y == null) return vol;
169	    const d = Math.hypot(x - this.lx, y - this.ly);
170	    if (d >= Sfx.ATTEN) return 0;
171	    return vol * (1 - d / Sfx.ATTEN);
172	  }
173	
174	  private ensure(silent = false): AudioContext | null {
175	    if (this.muted && !silent) return null;
176	    if (!this.ctx) {
177	      const AC = window.AudioContext ?? (window as unknown as { webkitAudioContext: typeof AudioContext }).webkitAudioContext;
178	      if (!AC) return null;
179	      this.ctx = new AC();
180	    }
181	    if (this.ctx.state === 'suspended') this.ctx.resume().catch(() => {});
182	    return this.ctx;
183	  }
184	
185	  /** 播放原版 wav（随机变体 + 轻微音高扰动）；vol 0-1 */
186	  playWav(name: SfxName, vol = 1, x?: number, y?: number): boolean {
187	    const list = WAV_MAP[name];
188	    if (!list || !list.length) return false;
189	    return this.playWavFile(list[Math.floor(Math.random() * list.length)], vol, x, y);
190	  }
191	
192	  /** 播放任意 wav 文件列表（随机选 + 音高扰动）；供怪物专属声效用 */
193	  playFiles(files: string[], vol = 1, x?: number, y?: number): boolean {
194	    if (!files.length) return false;
195	    return this.playWavFile(files[Math.floor(Math.random() * files.length)], vol, x, y);
196	  }
197	
198	  /** 按文件名直播（缓冲未就位时触发后台加载，本播返回 false 走合成兜底） */
199	  playWavFile(file: string, vol = 1, x?: number, y?: number): boolean {
200	    const ctx = this.ensure();
201	    if (!ctx) return false;
202	    const v = this.atten(vol, x, y);
203	    if (v <= 0) return false;
204	    vol = v;
205	    const buf = this.ensureBuffer(file);
206	    if (!buf) return false;
207	    const src = ctx.createBufferSource();
208	    src.buffer = buf;
209	    src.playbackRate.value = 0.92 + Math.random() * 0.16;
210	    const g = ctx.createGain();
211	    g.gain.value = Math.min(1, vol * this.master);
212	    src.connect(g).connect(ctx.destination);
213	    src.start();
214	    return true;
215	  }
216	
217	  play(name: SfxName, vol = 1, x?: number, y?: number) {
218	    // 优先原版素材，失败回退合成（master 在 playWavFile/osc 各自生效一次）。
219	    // 带坐标时衰减（atten），≤0 直接不播——合成回退也不触发
220	    if (x != null && y != null && this.atten(vol, x, y) <= 0) return;
221	    if (this.playWav(name, vol, x, y)) return;
222	    const ctx = this.ensure();
223	    if (!ctx) return;
224	    const t = ctx.currentTime;
225	    const osc = (type: OscillatorType, f0: number, f1: number, dur: number, vol: number, delay = 0) => {
226	      const o = ctx.createOscillator();
227	      const g = ctx.createGain();
228	      o.type = type;
229	      o.frequency.setValueAtTime(f0, t + delay);
230	      o.frequency.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(Math.max(1, f1), t + delay + dur);
231	      g.gain.setValueAtTime(vol * this.master, t + delay);
232	      g.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.001, t + delay + dur);
233	      o.connect(g).connect(ctx.destination);
234	      o.start(t + delay);
235	      o.stop(t + delay + dur);
236	    };
237	    switch (name) {
238	      case 'hit': osc('square', 320, 110, 0.09, 0.12); break;
239	      case 'chop': osc('triangle', 220, 80, 0.12, 0.16); break;
240	      case 'hurt': osc('sawtooth', 180, 60, 0.22, 0.14); break;
241	      case 'killed': osc('square', 260, 60, 0.25, 0.14); break;
242	      case 'pkilled': osc('sawtooth', 160, 40, 0.6, 0.18); break;
243	      case 'zombie': osc('sawtooth', 90, 55, 0.5, 0.1); break;
244	      case 'roar': osc('sawtooth', 70, 40, 0.9, 0.2); break;
245	      case 'pickup': osc('sine', 520, 990, 0.09, 0.1); osc('sine', 780, 1320, 0.08, 0.06, 0.05); break;
246	      case 'dig': osc('square', 140, 90, 0.05, 0.06); break;
247	      case 'tink': osc('sine', 900, 500, 0.06, 0.08); break;
248	      case 'place': osc('triangle', 260, 120, 0.08, 0.12); break;
249	      case 'drink': osc('sine', 300, 620, 0.06, 0.08); osc('sine', 420, 780, 0.06, 0.07, 0.07); osc('sine', 560, 940, 0.06, 0.06, 0.14); break;
250	      case 'splash': osc('sine', 700, 200, 0.18, 0.1); break;
251	      case 'summon': {
252	        // 召唤杖 UseSound(Item_44)近似:短促"噗"——高频下扫正弦 + 一撮噪声气声
253	        osc('sine', 900, 260, 0.16, 0.14);
254	        osc('triangle', 1400, 500, 0.08, 0.06);
255	        break;
256	      }
257	      case 'whipCrack': {
258	        // 鞭响(Item_152)近似:高频噪声急促炸开 + 快速衰减
259	        const wDur = 0.12, wn = Math.floor(ctx.sampleRate * wDur);
260	        const wBuf = ctx.createBuffer(1, wn, ctx.sampleRate);
261	        const wd = wBuf.getChannelData(0);
262	        for (let i = 0; i < wn; i++) wd[i] = (Math.random() * 2 - 1) * Math.pow(1 - i / wn, 3);
263	        const wSrc = ctx.createBufferSource();
264	        wSrc.buffer = wBuf;
265	        const hp = ctx.createBiquadFilter();
266	        hp.type = 'highpass';
267	        hp.frequency.value = 1200;
268	        const wg = ctx.createGain();
269	        wg.gain.value = 0.18 * this.master;
270	        wSrc.connect(hp).connect(wg).connect(ctx.destination);
271	        wSrc.start(t);
272	        break;
273	      }
274	      case 'explosion': {
275	        // 噪声爆发近似(原版 Item_14):一次性白噪 buffer + 低频 thump。
276	        // ★曾无此分支:按需加载首播(wav 还在 fetch)完全静音 = "炸弹没有音效"主因
277	        const dur = 0.5, n = Math.floor(ctx.sampleRate * dur);
278	        const buf = ctx.createBuffer(1, n, ctx.sampleRate);
279	        const d = buf.getChannelData(0);
280	        for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) d[i] = (Math.random() * 2 - 1) * Math.pow(1 - i / n, 2.2);
281	        const src = ctx.createBufferSource();
282	        src.buffer = buf;
283	        const lp = ctx.createBiquadFilter();
284	        lp.type = 'lowpass';
285	        lp.frequency.setValueAtTime(900, t);
286	        lp.frequency.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(120, t + dur);
287	        const g = ctx.createGain();
288	        g.gain.value = 0.5 * this.master;
289	        src.connect(lp).connect(g).connect(ctx.destination);
290	        src.start(t);
291	        osc('sine', 90, 35, 0.35, 0.35); // 低频冲击
292	        break;
293	      }
294	      case 'coin': osc('sine', 990, 1400, 0.07, 0.08); osc('sine', 1320, 1760, 0.09, 0.06, 0.05); break;
295	      case 'door_open': osc('triangle', 180, 320, 0.12, 0.1); break;
296	      case 'door_close': osc('triangle', 320, 160, 0.1, 0.1); break;
297	      case 'shatter': osc('square', 1400, 300, 0.15, 0.1); break;
298	      case 'drown': osc('sine', 400, 90, 0.4, 0.1); break;
299	    }
300	  }
301	}
302

Now the rotation helper rewrite (item 8):

```ts
/** EoC 旋转追踪（cs:19975-20012）：线性步进 ±rate（最短弧）+ 吸附带（|Δ|<rate 直接吸附） */
private applyEocRotation(target: number, rate: number): void {
  let t = target;
  while (t < 0) t += Math.PI * 2;
  while (t >= Math.PI * 2) t -= Math.PI * 2;
  let a = this.visAngle;
  while (a < 0) a += Math.PI * 2;
  while (a >= Math.PI * 2) a -= Math.PI * 2;
  if (rate <= 0) { this.visAngle = a; return; }   // flag3&&expert:num8=0 冻结朝向
  if (a < t) {
    if (t - a > Math.PI) a -= rate; else a += rate;
  } else if (a > t) {
    if (a - t > Math.PI) a += rate; else a -= rate;
  }
  // 环绕归一 + 吸附带(cs:19997-20011 两次判定)
  if (a < 0) a += Math.PI * 2; else if (a >= Math.PI * 2) a -= Math.PI * 2;
  if (a > t - rate && a < t + rate) a = t;
  this.visAngle = a;
}
```
Note vanilla uses 3.1415 (not π) for the wrap decision and 6.283 for normalization — copy those literals? The difference matters only at exact ±π. I'll use Math.PI for readability — the 3.1415 vs π distinction never matters in practice. Hmm, "数值一律取最终态" — 3.1415 is the literal. Hmm wait — the velocity small-clamp: vanilla `if (velocity.X > -0.1 && velocity.X < 0.1) velocity.X = 0;` — strict, inside the friction branch. I'll copy exactly: `if (this.vx > -0.1 && this.vx < 0.1) this.vx = 0;`.

Also — the movement + clamp at the end: currently done once after the switch. With my structure (P1 branch / transform branch / P2 branch), I'll do movement+clamp once at the end of eocAI (after the three-way dispatch) — but the transform branch and P1 both `return` in vanilla... vanilla returns after each branch, and movement happens in the caller (vanilla movement is global, not in the AI). In our repo, eocAI does the movement itself (EoC ignores terrain). So: structure as if/else-if branches, then a single shared movement+clamp at the end. Vanilla returns don't affect movement (it's outside AI in vanilla). ✓

But careful: the P1 branch has the transform-threshold check at the end (inside `if (ai0===0)`). And after the transform triggers, vanilla returns (P1 branch ends) — movement still happens globally. In my structure, after the threshold check I fall to the shared movement. ✓ equivalent.

Note: when the transform triggers mid-dash (hp drops below threshold during state 2), vanilla: ai0=1, ai1..3=0 — and the velocity is NOT zeroed (the current repo code zeroes vx/vy at transform entry — line 3818 `this.vx = 0; this.vy = 0;` — that's a deviation! Vanilla keeps velocity and damps ×0.98 per tick in the transform). Remove the zeroing. Also the current code spawns 20 particles at transform entry (line 3819) — vanilla doesn't (particles come at stage-1 end). Remove.

150 : 130;\n        if (this.aiT >= dashDur) {\n          this.chargesLeft++;\n          this.aiT = 0;\n          if (this.chargesLeft >= 3) {\n            this.state = 0; this.chargesLeft = 0;   // 3 连结束回悬浮(cs:20212-20216/20518-20521)\n          } else {\n            this.state = 1;                          // 再冲\n          }\n        }\n        break;\n      }\n      case 5: {\n        // ---- 自旋变身(cs:20238-20344):★单段加速 100t(ai2 +=0.005 钳 0.5,\n        //     速度 0.98 阻尼)。100t 到点【立即变身】P2:SoundID 3 + gore×6 +\n        //     尘×20 + 吼声(15)——残余自旋在 P2 悬浮中按 -0.005/帧 衰减\n        //     (原版\"边转边停\")。曾做成 加速100t+减速100t 共 200t 才变身,节奏翻倍 ----\n        this.spinSpeed = Math.min(0.5, this.spinSpeed + 0.005);\n        this.visAngle += this.spinSpeed;\n        this.vx *= 0.98; this.vy *= 0.98;\n        if (this.aiT >= 100) {\n          this.aiT = 0;\n          this.state = 0;                       // 直接进 P2 悬浮(cs:20309)\n          this.spinPhase = 0;\n          game.playSfx('roar');                 // SoundID 15\n          game.spawnParticles(this.cx, this.cy, '#8A1A1A', 12, 3);   // gore 3×2 份\n          game.spawnParticles(this.cx, this.cy, '#FF3030', 20, 2.5); // 尘 20\n        }\n        break;\n      }\n      default: {\n        this.state = 0; this.aiT = 0;\n        break;\n      }\n    }\n\n    // ---- 悬浮/非冲刺态的旋转追踪(最短弧;速率分档) ----\n    if (this.state !== 2 && this.state !== 5) {\n      this.applyEocRotation(Math.atan2(dy, dx) - Math.PI * 0.5, rotRate);\n    }\n\n    // 移动：EoC 全程无视地形——悬浮飞行体贴图块边缘会卡顿、冲撞后残留在\n    // 图块内也会受碰撞影响，统一直接积分 + 世界边界钳制（撞墙/嵌入问题全部消除）\n    this.x += this.vx; this.y += this.vy;\n    this.onGround = false; this.hitWall = false; this.hitHead = false;\n    const maxX = game.world.w * T - this.w, maxY = game.world.h * T - this.h;\n    this.x = Math.max(0, Math.min(maxX, this.x));\n    this.y = Math.max(0, Math.min(maxY, this.y));\n  }",
 "new_string": "  private eocAI(game: GameHooks, player: Player | null) {\n    const T = TILE;\n    if (!player) return;\n    const dx = player.cx - this.cx, dy = player.cy - this.cy;\n    const dist = Math.hypot(dx, dy) || 1;\n    // Main.Difficulty / Main.expertMode（:19903 起；getGoodWorld 抬档同义）\n    const diff = mainDifficulty(game.world);\n    const expert = mainExpertMode(game.world);\n    // flag2/flag3（:19906-19915，专家档专用）：hp<12% / hp<4%\n    const flag2 = expert && this.hp < this.maxHp * 0.12;\n    const flag3 = expert && this.hp < this.maxHp * 0.04;\n    // num4（:19917-19921）：连冲冲程，狂暴档减半\n    let num4 = 20;\n    if (flag3) num4 = 10;\n\n    // num7（:19927-19934）：脸向角 = atan2(player→NPC) + π/2 ≡ atan2(NPC→player) − π/2\n    const face = Math.atan2(dy, dx) - Math.PI * 0.5;\n\n    // ---- 旋转追踪速率表（:19939-19966）：默认 0；专家 ×1.5；狂暴归 0 锁脸 ----\n    // 此前恒 0.02 起步 + 悬浮 40t 抬 0.05——与原版分档表不符（悬浮 40t 档属冲刺态）\n    let rotRate = 0;\n    if (this.ai0 === 0 && this.state === 0) rotRate = 0.02;\n    if (this.ai0 === 0 && this.state === 2 && this.ai2 > 40) rotRate = 0.05;\n    if (this.ai0 === 3 && this.state === 0) rotRate = 0.05;\n    if (this.ai0 === 3 && this.state === 2 && this.ai2 > 40) rotRate = 0.08;\n    if (this.ai0 === 3 && this.state === 4 && this.ai2 > num4) rotRate = 0.15;\n    if (this.ai0 === 3 && this.state === 5) rotRate = 0.05;\n    if (expert) rotRate *= 1.5;\n    if (flag3 && expert) rotRate = 0;\n    this.applyEocRotation(face, rotRate);\n\n    // ---- 白天/玩家死亡：向上漂离 + EncourageDespawn(10)（:20020-20025） ----\n    // 原版在旋转段之后 return：状态机与 ai[2] 全部冻结，复活后从冻结处继续\n    if (game.world.clock.isDay || player.dead) {\n      this.vy -= 0.04;\n      this.encourageDespawn(10);\n      this.x += this.vx; this.y += this.vy;\n      if (dist > T * 120) this.dead = true;   // 漂远后兜底消失\n      return;\n    }\n\n    if (this.ai0 === 0) {\n      // ================= P1（ai[0]==0，:20026-20236） =================\n      if (this.state === 0) {\n        // ---- 悬浮伺服（:20030-20081）：5/0.04；专家 7/0.15（getGoodWorld +1/+0.05 不实现） ----\n        this.dashing = false;\n        let num10 = 5, num11 = 0.04;\n        if (expert) { num11 = 0.15; num10 = 7; }\n        this.servoTo(player.cx, player.cy - 200, num10, num11);\n        this.facing = dx > 0 ? 1 : -1;\n        this.ai2 += 1;\n        // 悬浮计时 600t 出冲（:20083-20095；专家 ×0.35 = 210）\n        let num16 = 600;\n        if (expert) num16 *= 0.35;\n        if (this.ai2 >= num16) {\n          this.state = 1; this.ai2 = 0; this.chargesLeft = 0; this.servantT = 0;\n        } else if ((this.y + this.h < player.y && dist < 500) || (expert && dist < 500)) {\n          // 仆从累计（:20096-20111）：NPC 在玩家上方且 <500px（专家仅距离门），\n          // 110t（专家 ×0.4 = 44）召一只，速度 5 / 专家 6\n          if (!player.dead) this.servantT += 1;\n          let num17 = 110;\n          if (expert) num17 *= 0.4;\n          if (this.servantT >= num17) {\n            this.servantT = 0;\n            const num18 = expert ? 6 : 5;\n            const s = Enemy.fromVanilla(5, this.cx + (dx / dist) * 10, this.cy + (dy / dist) * 10);\n            if (s) {\n              s.vx = (dx / dist) * num18;\n              s.vy = (dy / dist) * num18;\n              (game as unknown as { entities: { add: (e: unknown, b: string) => void } }).entities.add(s, 'enemies');\n              // SoundID 3 = NPCHit（:20140，原版在仆从落点播，非召唤音）\n              game.playSfxFiles(['NPC_Hit_1'], 1, this.cx, this.cy);\n              game.spawnParticles(s.cx, s.cy, '#9A8FA0', 10, 2);\n            }\n          }\n        }\n      } else if (this.state === 1) {\n        // ---- 冲刺预备（:20148-20169）：锁脸即发，速 6 / 专家 7 ----\n        this.visAngle = face;\n        const num23 = expert ? 7 : 6;\n        this.vx = (dx / dist) * num23;\n        this.vy = (dy / dist) * num23;\n        this.state = 2; this.ai2 = 0;\n        this.dashing = true;\n      } else if (this.state === 2) {\n        // ---- 冲刺（:20170-20222）：40t 后 0.98 摩擦（专家再 ×0.985）；冲程 150 / 专家 100 ----\n        this.dashing = true;\n        if (this.ai2 % 2 === 0) {\n          game.spawnParticles(this.cx, this.cy, '#D0D0D0', 2, 2);\n        }\n        this.ai2 += 1;\n        if (this.ai2 >= 40) {\n          this.vx *= 0.98; this.vy *= 0.98;\n          if (expert) { this.vx *= 0.985; this.vy *= 0.985; }\n          if (this.vx > -0.1 && this.vx < 0.1) this.vx = 0;\n          if (this.vy > -0.1 && this.vy < 0.1) this.vy = 0;\n        } else {\n          // 冲刺前 40t 旋转锁移动方向（:20195）\n          this.visAngle = Math.atan2(this.vy, this.vx) - Math.PI * 0.5;\n        }\n        const num27 = expert ? 100 : 150;\n        if (this.ai2 >= num27) {\n          this.chargesLeft += 1;\n          this.ai2 = 0;\n          this.visAngle = face;\n          if (this.chargesLeft >= 3) {\n            this.state = 0; this.chargesLeft = 0;   // 3 连结束回悬浮（:20212-20216）\n          } else {\n            this.state = 1;                          // 再冲\n          }\n        }\n      } else {\n        this.state = 0; this.ai2 = 0; this.chargesLeft = 0;\n      }\n      // 变身阈值（:20223-20235）：hp < 50%（专家 65%）→ 段一加速自旋（速度不清零，\n      // 由变身段 0.98/t 阻尼自然收尾——原版语义）\n      const num28 = expert ? 0.65 : 0.5;\n      if (this.hp < this.maxHp * num28) {\n        this.ai0 = 1; this.state = 0; this.ai2 = 0;\n        this.chargesLeft = 0; this.servantT = 0;\n        this.spinSpeed = 0;\n        this.phase = 2;                 // 仓内 umbrella：变身起即二阶段（legacy 渲染消费）\n        this.dashing = false;\n      }\n    } else if (this.ai0 === 1 || this.ai0 === 2) {\n      // ================= 变身两段（ai[0]==1/2，:20238-20344） =================\n      // 段一（ai0==1）加速：ai[2] +=0.005 钳 0.5；段二（ai0==2）减速：-0.005 钳 0\n      // （getGoodWorld 再循环 ai[3]==1 走加速支，不实现）\n      this.dashing = false;\n      if (this.ai0 === 1) {\n        this.spinSpeed = Math.min(0.5, this.spinSpeed + 0.005);\n      } else {\n        this.spinSpeed = Math.max(0, this.spinSpeed - 0.005);\n      }\n      this.visAngle += this.spinSpeed;\n      this.ai2 += 1;                    // 原 ai[1]：段内计时\n      // 专家自旋期每 20t 甩一只仆从（:20262-20300）：随机向 ±200 归一到速 5、落点 +10\n      if (expert && this.ai2 % 20 === 0) {\n        const rx = Math.random() * 400 - 200, ry = Math.random() * 400 - 200;\n        const rl = Math.hypot(rx, ry) || 1;\n        const s = Enemy.fromVanilla(5, this.cx + (rx / rl) * 10, this.cy + (ry / rl) * 10);\n        if (s) {\n          s.vx = (rx / rl) * 5;\n          s.vy = (ry / rl) * 5;\n          (game as unknown as { entities: { add: (e: unknown, b: string) => void } }).entities.add(s, 'enemies');\n          game.spawnParticles(s.cx, s.cy, '#9A8FA0', 10, 2);\n        }\n      }\n      if (this.ai2 >= 100) {\n        this.ai0 += 1;\n        this.ai2 = 0;\n        if (this.ai0 === 3) {\n          // 段二满 100t → P2（:20312-20315）：ai[2] 清零收住残余自旋\n          this.spinSpeed = 0;\n        } else {\n          // 段一满 100t（:20316-20330）：NPCHit(3) + gore 8/7/6 ×2 + 尘 20 + Roar(15)\n          game.playSfxFiles(['NPC_Hit_1'], 1, this.cx, this.cy);\n          game.playSfx('roar');\n          game.spawnParticles(this.cx, this.cy, '#8A1A1A', 12, 3);   // gore 8/7/6 ×2 份\n          game.spawnParticles(this.cx, this.cy, '#FF3030', 20, 2.5); // 尘 20\n        }\n      }\n      // 速度 0.98/t 阻尼 + 小值清零（:20333-20343）\n      this.vx *= 0.98; this.vy *= 0.98;\n      if (this.vx > -0.1 && this.vx < 0.1) this.vx = 0;\n      if (this.vy > -0.1 && this.vy < 0.1) this.vy = 0;\n    } else if (this.ai0 === 3) {\n      // ================= P2（ai[0]==3，:20346-20756） =================\n      // ---- 伤害/防御每 tick 覆写（:20346-20362，猪鲨同款先例） ----\n      // legacy 路径 def 是 ENEMY_DEFS 共享引用——覆写前先拷成实例私有（vanilla 路径\n      // fromVanilla 已是拷贝，恒等判断最多拷一次）\n      if (this.def === (ENEMY_DEFS[this.key] ?? PLACEHOLDER_DEF)) this.def = { ...this.def };\n      this.def.defense = 0;\n      let num38 = 18;\n      if (expert) {\n        if (flag2) this.def.defense = -15;                      // 专家 hp<12%\n        if (flag3) { num38 = 20; this.def.defense = -30; }      // 专家 hp<4%\n      }\n      // GetAttackDamage_LerpBetweenFinalValues(23, num38)（:7026 = Remap Classic→Expert）\n      // 再 CappedAtMaster（:7020）：经典 23 / 专家 36 / 大师 54\n      this.def.damage = getAttackDamageCappedAtMaster(\n        Math.trunc(remap(diff, GDL.CLASSIC, GDL.EXPERT, 23, num38)), diff);\n\n      // 专家 hp<12% 起手即走\"悬下方回冲\"（:20363-20366；ai[2] 不清零为原版怪癖）\n      if (this.state === 0 && flag2) this.state = 5;\n\n      if (this.state === 0) {\n        // ---- 悬浮（:20367-20430）：6/0.07；专家按 400/600/800px 距离爬坡 +1/+0.05 ----\n        this.dashing = false;\n        let num39 = 6, num40 = 0.07;\n        if (dist > 400 && expert) {\n          num39 += 1; num40 += 0.05;\n          if (dist > 600) {\n            num39 += 1; num40 += 0.05;\n            if (dist > 800) { num39 += 1; num40 += 0.05; }\n          }\n        }\n        this.servoTo(player.cx, player.cy - 120, num39, num40);\n        this.facing = dx > 0 ? 1 : -1;\n        this.ai2 += 1;\n        if (this.ai2 >= 200) {\n          this.state = 1; this.ai2 = 0; this.chargesLeft = 0;\n          if (expert && this.hp < this.maxHp * 0.35) this.state = 3;   // :20436-20439 → 连冲\n        }\n        if (expert && flag3) {\n          // :20443-20450 狂暴：直接转连冲，ai[3] −1000（计数远不达 5 → 持续连冲）\n          this.state = 3; this.ai2 = 0; this.chargesLeft -= 1000;\n        }\n      } else if (this.state === 1) {\n        // ---- 冲刺预备（:20452-20478）：ForceRoar(36)；6.8（专家第 2/3 冲 ×1.15/×1.30） ----\n        game.playSfx('roar', 0.7);\n        this.visAngle = face;\n        let num44 = 6.8;\n        if (expert && this.chargesLeft === 1) num44 *= 1.15;\n        if (expert && this.chargesLeft === 2) num44 *= 1.3;\n        this.vx = (dx / dist) * num44;\n        this.vy = (dy / dist) * num44;\n        this.state = 2; this.ai2 = 0;\n        this.dashing = true;\n      } else if (this.state === 2) {\n        // ---- 冲刺（:20479-20534）：50t(专家)后 0.97 摩擦(+专家 ×0.98)；冲程 130 / 专家 90 ----\n        this.dashing = true;\n        if (this.ai2 % 2 === 0) {\n          game.spawnParticles(this.cx, this.cy, '#FF4040', 2, 2);\n        }\n        this.ai2 += 1;\n        const num48 = expert ? 50 : 40;\n        if (this.ai2 >= num48) {\n          this.vx *= 0.97; this.vy *= 0.97;\n          if (expert) { this.vx *= 0.98; this.vy *= 0.98; }\n          if (this.vx > -0.1 && this.vx < 0.1) this.vx = 0;\n          if (this.vy > -0.1 && this.vy < 0.1) this.vy = 0;\n        } else {\n          this.visAngle = Math.atan2(this.vy, this.vx) - Math.PI * 0.5;\n        }\n        const num49 = expert ? 90 : 130;\n        if (this.ai2 >= num49) {\n          this.chargesLeft += 1;\n          this.ai2 = 0;\n          this.visAngle = face;\n          if (this.chargesLeft >= 3) {\n            this.state = 0; this.chargesLeft = 0;   // 3 连结束回悬浮（:20518-20521）\n            if (expert && this.hp < this.maxHp * 0.5) {\n              // :20522-20526 专家 hp<50% → 预测连冲，起手计数 +Next(1,4)\n              this.state = 3;\n              this.chargesLeft += 1 + Math.floor(Math.random() * 3);\n            }\n          } else {\n            this.state = 1;                          // 再冲\n          }\n        }\n      } else if (this.state === 3) {\n        // ---- 预测连冲预备（:20535-20644，专家专属态）：带玩家速度提前量直冲 ----\n        if (this.chargesLeft === 4 && flag2 && this.cy > player.cy) {\n          // :20537-20544 已连冲 4 次且漂到玩家下方 → 回悬浮（防无限连冲）\n          this.state = 0; this.ai2 = 0; this.chargesLeft = 0;\n          this.dashing = false;\n        } else {\n          let num50 = 20;                  // 冲速\n          let num51 = dx, num52 = dy;      // 指向玩家\n          // 提前量系数（:20552-20561）：|pvx|+|pvy|/4 再 +=10−self ≡ 恒 10（钳 5..15 原文照抄）\n          let num53 = Math.abs(player.vx) + Math.abs(player.vy) / 4;\n          num53 += 10 - num53;\n          if (num53 < 5) num53 = 5;\n          if (num53 > 15) num53 = 15;\n          if (this.ai2 === -1 && !flag3) { num53 *= 4; num50 *= 1.3; }   // :20562-20566 悬下方回冲\n          if (flag3) num53 *= 2;                                          // :20567-20570 狂暴双倍\n          num51 -= player.vx * num53;\n          num52 -= player.vy * num53 / 4;\n          num51 *= 1 + (Math.random() * 21 - 10) * 0.01;                  // :20573-20574 ±10% 散布\n          num52 *= 1 + (Math.random() * 21 - 10) * 0.01;\n          if (flag3) {\n            num51 *= 1 + (Math.random() * 21 - 10) * 0.01;\n            num52 *= 1 + (Math.random() * 21 - 10) * 0.01;\n          }\n          const num54 = Math.hypot(num51, num52) || 1;\n          const num55 = num54;\n          this.vx = (num51 / num54) * num50;\n          this.vy = (num52 / num54) * num50;\n          this.vx += (Math.random() * 41 - 20) * 0.1;                     // :20585-20586 ±2 抖动\n          this.vy += (Math.random() * 41 - 20) * 0.1;\n          if (flag3) {\n            // 狂暴（:20587-20607）：再抖 ±5 → 十字翻折（|v| 分量互换带侧向符号）→ 归一 ×num50\n            this.vx += (Math.random() * 101 - 50) * 0.1;\n            this.vy += (Math.random() * 101 - 50) * 0.1;\n            let num56 = Math.abs(this.vx), num57 = Math.abs(this.vy);\n            if (this.cx > player.cx) num57 *= -1;\n            if (this.cy > player.cy) num56 *= -1;\n            this.vx = num57 + this.vx;\n            this.vy = num56 + this.vy;\n            const vl = Math.hypot(this.vx, this.vy) || 1;\n            this.vx = (this.vx / vl) * num50;\n            this.vy = (this.vy / vl) * num50;\n            this.vx += (Math.random() * 41 - 20) * 0.1;\n            this.vy += (Math.random() * 41 - 20) * 0.1;\n          } else if (num55 < 100) {\n            // 近距（:20608-20625）：水平冲 → 垂直翻折（v 分量互换带侧向符号）\n            if (Math.abs(this.vx) > Math.abs(this.vy)) {\n              let num58 = Math.abs(this.vx), num59 = Math.abs(this.vy);\n              if (this.cx > player.cx) num59 *= -1;\n              if (this.cy > player.cy) num58 *= -1;\n              this.vx = num59;\n              this.vy = num58;\n            }\n          } else if (Math.abs(this.vx) > Math.abs(this.vy)) {\n            // 远距水平（:20626-20640）：斜 45° 折冲\n            let num60 = (Math.abs(this.vx) + Math.abs(this.vy)) / 2;\n            let num61 = num60;\n            if (this.cx > player.cx) num61 *= -1;\n            if (this.cy > player.cy) num60 *= -1;\n            this.vx = num61;\n            this.vy = num60;\n          }\n          this.state = 4;\n          this.dashing = true;\n        }\n      } else if (this.state === 4) {\n        // ---- 连冲（:20645-20698）：冲程 num4（20 / 狂暴 10），+13t 摩擦尾，5 连后回悬浮 ----\n        if (this.ai2 === 0) game.playSfx('roar', 0.7);   // SoundID 36 ForceRoar（:20647-20650）\n        this.dashing = true;\n        if (this.ai2 % 2 === 0) {\n          game.spawnParticles(this.cx, this.cy, '#FF4040', 2, 2);\n        }\n        const num62 = num4;\n        this.ai2 += 1;\n        if (this.ai2 === num62 && Math.hypot(this.x - player.x, this.y - player.y) < 200) {\n          this.ai2 -= 1;   // :20653-20656 贴脸 <200px 原地延时一格\n        }\n        if (this.ai2 >= num62) {\n          this.vx *= 0.95; this.vy *= 0.95;\n          if (this.vx > -0.1 && this.vx < 0.1) this.vx = 0;\n          if (this.vy > -0.1 && this.vy < 0.1) this.vy = 0;\n        } else {\n          this.visAngle = Math.atan2(this.vy, this.vx) - Math.PI * 0.5;\n        }\n        const num63 = num62 + 13;\n        if (this.ai2 >= num63) {\n          this.chargesLeft += 1;\n          this.ai2 = 0;\n          if (this.chargesLeft >= 5) {\n            this.state = 0; this.chargesLeft = 0;   // 5 连结束回悬浮（:20679-20682）\n            this.dashing = false;\n            // getGoodWorld 视线内再自旋循环（:20683-20691）不实现\n          } else {\n            this.state = 3;                          // 继续连冲\n          }\n        }\n      } else if (this.state === 5) {\n        // ---- 悬玩家下方 600px（:20699-20752，专家 hp<12% 狂暴态）：9/0.3 快伺服 ----\n        this.dashing = false;\n        this.servoTo(player.cx, player.cy + 600, 9, 0.3);\n        this.facing = dx > 0 ? 1 : -1;\n        this.ai2 += 1;\n        if (this.ai2 >= 70) {\n          this.state = 3;\n          this.ai2 = -1; this.ai2 = 0; this.chargesLeft = 0;\n      }\n      // 狂暴档不允许停留悬下方态（:20753-20755）\n      if (flag3 && this.state === 5) this.state = 3;\n    }\n\n    // 移动：EoC 全程无视地形——悬浮飞行体贴图块边缘会卡顿、冲撞后残留在\n    // 图块内也会受碰撞影响，统一直接积分 + 世界边界钳制（撞墙/嵌入问题全部消除）\n    this.x += this.vx; (frameIdx < 3 ? 23 : 30) : 0;\n        ctx.drawImage(r.img, r.sx, r.sy, r.sw, r.sh, -r.sw / 2, anchorY + eocOff, r.sw, r.sh);\n        // ---- EoC 冲刺残影（Main.cs:25469-25481 1:1）：门 = ai[0]==3 且 ai[1]≥4\n        //      （P2 连冲态 4 / 悬下方回冲态 5）→ 沿 oldPos 画 9 层鬼影，每层 RGBA\n        //      × 0.5×(10−i)/20（越旧越淡），同一帧/旋转，锚点同本体（中心+eocOff）。\n        //      原版该分支在 DrawNPC 本体 Draw 之后求值 → 鬼影压在本体之上（越近越深）；\n        //      此前近似门 dashing && phase≥2 把普通 3 连冲也画了鬼影且画在本体之前。\n        //      仓内映射：ai0=ai[0]、state=ai[1]（见 Enemy.eocAI 头注）----\n        if (e.vanillaId === 4 && e.ai0 === 3 && e.state >= 4) {\n          for (let i = 1; i < 10; i++) {\n            const depth = e.historyDepth();\n            if (i >= depth) break;\n            const ga = (10 - i) / 40;   // 0.5 × (10-i)/20\n            if (ga <= 0.01) continue;\n            ctx.save();\n            ctx.translate(e.histXAt(i) + e.w / 2, e.histYAt(i) + e.h / 2);\n            ctx.rotate(e.visAngle);\n            ctx.scale(scale, scale);\n            if (!rotationDriven && e.facing > 0) ctx.scale(-1, 1);\n            ctx.globalAlpha = ga * (1 - Math.min(255, Math.max(e.spawnAlpha, 0)) / 255);\n            ctx.drawImage(r.img, r.sx, r.sy, r.sw, r.sh, -r.sw / 2, -r.sh / 2 + eocOff, r.sw, r.sh);\n            ctx.restore();\n          }\n          ctx.globalAlpha = 1 - Math.min(255, Math.max(e.spawnAlpha, 0)) / 255;\n        }"
}

 "old_string": "  useSuspiciousEye() {\n    if (this.world.clock.isDay) {\n      this.cb.onToast(Lang.text('Mods.SandboxWorld.Toast.NightOnly'));\n      return;\n    }\n    if (this.boss) return;\n    // 从远处地表生成（离玩家 ~55 格），Boss 会自己飞过来\n    const side = Math.random() < 0.5 ? -1 : 1;\n    const tx = Math.floor((this.player.cx + side * 55 * TILE) / TILE);\n    let ty = 10;\n    for (; ty < this.world.h; ty++) {\n      if (this.world.store.type[this.world.store.idx(tx, ty)] !== 0) break;\n    }\n    const spawnY = Math.max(4, ty - 12) * TILE;\n    // fromVanilla(4) 取原版 NPC_4 贴图(6 帧)+rotation;数值与 ENEMY_DEFS 一致(2800/15/12/0)\n    const e = Enemy.fromVanilla(4, tx * TILE + 8, spawnY);\n    if (e) {\n      e.id = this.entities.nextId++;\n      this.entities.add(e, 'enemies');\n      this.boss = e;\n    }\n    // 公告+音效与 summonBoss 同语义（EoC → \"克苏鲁之眼已苏醒！\" + Roar）\n    this.announceBossSummon(4);\n  }",
 "new_string": "  /** @returns 是否真正消耗（被召唤门拦下/白天时不消耗物品） */\n  useSuspiciousEye(): boolean {\n    if (this.world.clock.isDay) {\n      this.cb.onToast(Lang.text('Mods.SandboxWorld.Toast.NightOnly'));\n      return false;\n    }\n    // SummonItemCheck（Player.cs:41514-41526）：物品 43 只查场上【是否已有 NPC 4】——\n    // 与其他 Boss 是否在场无关（原版逐物品↔NPC 对照表 43↔4/70↔13/560↔50/…）。\n    // 拦下时 ItemCheck 置 flag=false：物品不使用也不消耗。此前误用 this.boss 全 Boss 门\n    // （其他 Boss 在场会白吞眼球）\n    if (this.entities.enemies.some((en) => {\n      const e2 = en as Enemy;\n      return !e2.dead && e2.vanillaId === 4;\n    })) return false;\n    // 从远处地表生成（离玩家 ~55 格），Boss 会自己飞过来\n    const side = Math.random() < 0.5 ? -1 : 1;\n    const tx = Math.floor((this.player.cx + side * 55 * TILE) / TILE);\n    let ty = 10;\n    for (; ty < this.world.h; ty++) {\n      if (this.world.store.type[this.world.store.idx(tx, ty)] !== 0) break;\n    }\n    const spawnY = Math.max(4, ty - 12) * TILE;\n    // fromVanilla(4) 取原版 NPC_4 贴图(6 帧)+rotation;数值与 ENEMY_DEFS 一致(2800/15/12/0)\n    const e = Enemy.fromVanilla(4, tx * TILE + 8, spawnY);\n    if (!e) return false;\n    e.id = this.entities.nextId++;\n    this.entities.add(e, 'enemies');\n    this.boss = e;\n    // 公告+音效与 summonBoss 同语义（EoC → \"克苏鲁之眼已苏醒！\" + Roar）\n    this.announceBossSummon(4);\n    return true;\n  }"
}
```

// 幽灵 parity 扫描：逐检查点对账 inactive 格的残留 type，
// 定位哪个 pass 首次写出与 C# oracle 不同的幽灵。
import { it } from 'vitest';
import { readFileSync } from 'node:fs';
import { runResetAndTerrain } from '../src/world/gen/WorldGen';
import { runDunesPass } from '../src/world/gen/vanilla/DunesPass';
import { runOceanSandPass } from '../src/world/gen/vanilla/BeachPass';
import { runSandPatchesPass, runTunnelsPass, runDirtWallBgPass, runSmallHolesPass } from '../src/world/gen/vanilla/MissingCavesPass';
import { runMountCavesPass, runMountainCavesPass } from '../src/world/gen/vanilla/MountCavesPass';
import { runRocksAndClayPass, runCavesPass } from '../src/world/gen/vanilla/CavesPass';
import { TILE_DEFS } from '../src/data/tiles';
import { fnv1a } from './pass-hash.test';

const V_ID = new Int32Array(TILE_DEFS.length).fill(-1);
for (let i = 0; i < TILE_DEFS.length; i++) {
  const sheet = TILE_DEFS[i]?.vanilla?.sheet;
  if (typeof sheet === 'number') V_ID[i] = sheet;
}
function gh(st: { type: Uint16Array; flags: Uint8Array; w: number; h: number }): number {
  const ghost = new Uint16Array(st.w * st.h);
  for (let i = 0; i < ghost.length; i++) {
    if (st.flags[i]) continue;
    const t = st.type[i];
    if (t === 0) { ghost[i] = 0; continue; } // 内部空 → vanilla 0（与 oracle 裸 typ 同口径）
    const v = V_ID[t];
    ghost[i] = v < 0 ? 0x7fff : v;
  }
  return fnv1a(ghost);
}

it('ghost parity per checkpoint', async () => {
  // oracle 侧逐检查点幽灵 dump：先只做 terrain（金标生成时存 typ 全量）

  runMountCavesPass(st, rng, gs); console.log('GHOST mountaincaves=' + gh(st).toString(16));
  runDirtWallBgPass(st, rng, gs); console.log('GHOST dirtwallbg=' + gh(st).toString(16));

  private eocAI(game: GameHooks, player: Player | null) {
    const T = TILE;
    if (!player) return;
    const dx = player.cx - this.cx, dy = player.cy - this.cy;
    const dist = Math.hypot(dx, dy) || 1;
    // Main.Difficulty / Main.expertMode（:19903 起；getGoodWorld 抬档同义）
    const diff = mainDifficulty(game.world);
    const expert = mainExpertMode(game.world);
    // flag2/flag3（:19906-19915，专家档专用）：hp<12% / hp<4%
    const flag2 = expert && this.hp < this.maxHp * 0.12;
    const flag3 = expert && this.hp < this.maxHp * 0.04;
    // num4（:19917-19921）：连冲冲程，狂暴档减半
